3. STABLE OPERATION
          3.1   Information Security


                Recognizing the paramount importance of information security and data protection, the Group strictly complies with the laws and
                regulations such as the Cybersecurity Law of the People’s Republic of China, the Provisions on Protection of Personal Information
                of Telecommunications and Internet Users and the Administrative Measures for Internet Information Services, and has established
                systems such as the Information Security System , the Regulations on Management of Data Backup and the Guideline on Emergency
                Response to Major Incidents . We uphold the information security policy of “putting safety first, focusing on comprehensive prevention,
                prioritizing prevention, and making continuous improvement”. Pursuant to the requirements of the ISO 27001 Information Security
                Management System Standard, we manage the information security management systems and related security work, including risk
                management, internal audit, and personnel security management, in an organized manner.

                To effectively operate the information security management system, the Group has developed a top-down organizational structure
                of information security management with clear responsibilities and authorities. Management representatives coordinate the overall
                information security arrangements, supported by an Information Security Management Committee composed of heads of all business
                departments. The Information Security Management Committee is responsible for finalizing the information security development
                strategy, overall planning, management system and major decisions, allocating resources to implement information security activities,
                and regularly reviewing management performance to ensure that the information security management system is applicable and
                effective. Under the Information Security Management Committee are an Information Security Executive Team and an Information
                Security Oversight Group. The Information Security Executive Team, led by the Security Center, is responsible for establishing,
                implementing, checking and improving the information security management system, implementing daily security protection work
                and annual risk assessment on the information system, and identifying and managing information security risks. The team consists
                of security contacts designated by each business department to facilitate the implementation with security requirements on the
                business side. Led by the Internal Audit Team, the Information Security Oversight Group mainly carries out information security
                audit and ensures compliance and effectiveness of various security measures from two perspectives: process auditing and security
                technology.

     The Group reinforces the information security defense from the two perspectives of personnel and infrastructure. To strengthen the
     standardized management of employee information security, we have formulated the Specification for Employee Information Security
     Management to outline employees’ code of conduct on this front and to improve their risk awareness in daily work. Meanwhile, we
     have established a data security control system, and optimized key processes such as data usage approval, desensitization procedure,
     and security detection for business launches. We work to raise organization-wide risk prevention awareness through information
     security training, thus safeguarding information assets of the Group and customers from all aspects. In terms of infrastructure, we
     have arranged dedicated personnel to be responsible for the daily maintenance, monitoring, alarming and troubleshooting of the
     server room environment to ensure the information security of users from the hardware and facility levels. Our server rooms are
     constructed in accordance with the Code for Design of Data Centers (GB50174–2017) and the Code for Construction and Acceptance
     of Data Center Infrastructure (GB50462–2015) , and have passed the relevant acceptance procedures.

     The Group’s information security management system and core business systems have obtained authoritative compliance
     certifications, aiming to strengthen the foundation for security protection. The TapTap platform and the XD Forum system have
     completed the Level 3 classification and filing for communication network security protection. The TapTap user system, the VeryCD
     official website, and the LeanCloud platform have all passed the Level 3 assessment of and filing for classified protection of
     cybersecurity.

     During the Year, the Group diligently honed its technical capabilities in information security and actively participated in industry
     collaborative governance. X.D. Network Inc. (“X.D. Network”), a subsidiary of the Group, was awarded the “Outstanding Techniques
     and Methods Award” and the “Outstanding Special Award” in the Shanghai Industry and Information Technology Network Security
     Attack and Defense Exercise “Rock Operation 2025”. In March 2025, the Group was actively involved in the “Clear Pujiang • Xuhui
     District Knowledge-Sharing Campaign for Better Online Business Environment and the Anti-Cybercrime Alliance Themed Workshop”
     organized by Xuhui District of Shanghai. As a core founding member of the “Anti-Cybercrime Alliance”, the Group participated in the
     formulation of and released in the event the Guidelines for Handling Cybercrime Issues (Version 2), thus empowering collaborative
     industry governance with practical experience, facilitating the fight against cybercrime, and contributing to a clearer online
     environment.

          3.2   Privacy Protection

                Ensuring user data security and protecting privacy are our core responsibilities. Due to the nature of our business, we receive,
                transmit and store players’ personal information and relevant data generated during their use in the course of our operations. The
                Group strictly complies with relevant laws and regulations, such as the Personal Information Protection Law of the People’s Republic
                of China . In addition, the Group has established a data protection system encompassing institutional norms, specialized protection
                and operational guidelines.

                The Group has formulated and implemented the Employee Confidentiality System , clarifying employees’ responsibilities and norms
                of behavior regarding data and information security. For underage users, the Group has stipulated the Protection Rules for Personal
                Information of Children on TapTap to strengthen specialized protection for children’s personal information. Furthermore, the
                Group has formulated and implemented the Compliance Operation Guidance for Personal Information Collection and Use on App ,
                standardizing the processes for collecting, using, storing, and transmitting personal information during business operations. These
                systems and guidelines constitute a management foundation for the Group’s data security and privacy protection, enhancing the
                compliance of personal information processing and mitigating associated risks.

                We have notified the scope, purpose, and authorized usage of personal information collected for each service in the Privacy Policy
                on the X.D. Network, TapTap platform, TapSDK, and other platforms. We have standardized the management throughout collection,
                storage, use, transfer, and disclosure of personal information, and clearly set out the subject rights and operational procedures for
                users to access, delete and duplicate personal data. By clarifying the rights and operational standards for modifying personal data, we
                protect privacy in accordance with established guidelines. During the Year, to align with the upgrade of advertising recommendation
                algorithms, we updated the Privacy Policy for the TapTap platform. In this way, we further specified the rules for using information
                related to personalized recommendations, safeguarded users’ personal information security, and refined the institutional norms for
                privacy protection.

                To safeguard users’ privacy rights, we protect their personal information through encryption, de-tagging, anonymization, and other
                techniques. Personal information modification and account deregistration are enabled on our platforms. We also provide a method
                for deleting or anonymizing users’ personal data once they have chosen to cancel their account.

                The Group attaches great importance to the risk control over full-process data security. We have deployed an automated security
                penetration testing platform and WAF security protection platform to manage risk at various nodes in the data lifecycle, such as
                establishing clear rules and obtaining user authorization during the collection of sensitive personal data; encryption and integrity
                verification during transmission; backup, recovery rehearsal, access control, encryption and retention of web logs during data
                storage; authorization approval, desensitization, watermark protection and interface output restrictions during data processing. By
                doing so, we strive to safeguard data security in a comprehensive manner.

                In terms of products, we have completed the App filing for our own games in accordance with regulatory requirements of the Ministry
                of Industry and Information Technology, and have been requiring the games on the TapTap platform to complete the relevant
                filing. As for user privacy rights, we have optimized the privacy settings for the platform’s personalized content and recommended
                advertising features. Meanwhile, we carry out self-assessment on user rights protection. The TapTap App, “Heartopia”, “Sausage
                Man”, “Bloons TD 6”, “Bamboo Rouge Shop”, and “Wizard of Legend” have all completed self-assessment on user rights protection
                and obtained approval, reinforcing our endeavors for secured user rights on the product side.

                During the Year, the Group did not violate any laws and regulations related to personal data.
48            ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND                                                                                                       XD INC.
                      GOVERNANCE REPORT                                                                                        ANNUAL REPORT 2025




     3.3   Marketing Compliance

           The Group expands the reach of its products through various forms of publicity and promotion techniques while adhering to the
           principles of compliance and information accuracy in marketing and publicity. We strictly adhere to relevant laws and regulations,
           such as the Advertising Law of the People’s Republic of China and the Administrative Measures for Internet Advertising, ensuring
           comprehensive compliance control over marketing information released across all channels.

           In managing advertising and promotional content, we pursue the pre-compliance principle to strictly review the compliance of
           external promotional content and regularly compile and update relevant industry laws, regulations, and regulatory requirements.
           The Marketing Department is responsible for the review of the Group’s promotional content, and the Legal Department and Public
           Relations Department make the final review of and decision on uncertain content. We ensure that our external output of information
           content is authentic, accurate, complete, and does not contain any deceptive or misleading statements. By doing so, we manage to
           prevent compliance risks from the source and protect the right to know and other legitimate interests of users.

           The Group has set up a systematic review and compliance assurance mechanism for advertisement placed by third parties on the
           TapTap platform. We review all forms of advertising content, including images, copy, and videos, to ensure the information is truthful,
           lawful, and free of infringement risks. As for qualification management, we examine the business license, proof of product ownership,
           software copyright certificate, game approval number, and other key qualifications of the advertiser and relevant party. Only after
           confirming that the product is legally authorized, we will publish the advertisement. Moreover, we require a letter of guarantee from
           the advertising agent in the framework contract and ask the legal representative of the agent to provide a joint liability guarantee, so
           as to minimize the risks of breaches and violations and strengthen risk control during cooperation. By standardizing the operation
           process of our advertising platform, strengthening the review of the advertiser authorization letter and authorization period, and
           implementing automated management such as authorization expiry reminders, we continue to improve the advertising review
           efficiency and enhance the compliance and transparency of advertising content. All of these efforts reflect our earnest practice of
           platform governance responsibilities.

           To strengthen employees’ compliance awareness and professional judgment and to fulfill requirements on advertising compliance
           management during business operations, the Group continuously conducts marketing and legal advocacy training. During the Year,
           we held advertising review training for the advertising review team and the advertising operation team, organized frequent issues in
           advertising qualification and content review, and conducted in-depth analysis of key and difficult issues. As such, we enhanced the
           compliance awareness and professional capabilities of responsible personnel.

           During the Year, the Group did not violate any laws and regulations related to advertising. Due to the nature of the Group’s business,
           requirements in respect of product labelling do not apply to the Group.

     3.4   Intellectual Property Rights


           Prioritizing the protection of intellectual property rights, the Group strictly abides by the Copyright Law of the People’s Republic of
           China , the Trademark Law of the People’s Republic of China , the Patent Law of the People’s Republic of China , the Measures for the
           Administration of Internet Domain Names of China and the Measures for the Administration of Software Products and other laws
           and regulations. We have formulated the Trademark Application Process , the Software Copyright Registration Application Process ,
           the Work Copyright Registration Application Process , the Patent Application Process , the Organizational Structure and Division of
           Responsibilities for Intellectual Property Protection , the Scope of Intellectual Property Rights and Attribution of Rights , and other
           internal management system, which collectively form standardized intellectual property management system.
XD INC.                                                                                              ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND                    49
ANNUAL REPORT 2025                                                                                   GOVERNANCE REPORT




            In support of innovation and commercialization of intellectual properties, we have formulated and implemented the XD Patent
            Management and Reward System to navigate employees through registration and application of intellectual property rights,
            standardize application of patents and other outcomes, and improve the process for protecting the intellectual property rights of
            various exclusive games. By doing so, we can promote the legitimate use of intellectual property rights. We provide appropriate
            bonuses and compensation to the inventor as an invention reward to encourage our employees to create inventions. In addition,
            to mitigate the risks associated with rights disputes, we have clarified the scope of intellectual property rights and the principle of
            attribution of rights. The intellectual property rights of creations organized, hosted, and completed by or on behalf of the Group, as
            well as the intellectual property rights generated by employees in the course of their duties, are attributed to the Group in principle,
            and the Group assumes the relevant management responsibilities.

            To effectively conclude our innovative achievements, we conduct registration and rights confirmation for patents, trademarks,
            software, and copyrights such as artwork, music, and text in accordance with the law. We have established a standardized intellectual
            property management process, and continuously optimize it in practice to enhance management efficiency. During the Year, we
            optimized management based on the structure and risk characteristics of our intellectual property rights. Given the large volume and
            frequent iteration of art assets, we moderately streamlined material submission and circulation processes to enhance convenience
            and team engagement. To address the risk of “trademark cancellation due to inactivity for three consecutive years”, we established a
            “proactive defense” system by identifying high-risk trademark categories, activating unused trademarks through third-party licensing
            cooperation, preserving lawful evidence of use, and building a comprehensive evidence repository. We did so to fight against external
            infringement and malicious cancellation.

            The Group is committed to safeguarding innovative achievements and market order, continuously strengthening intellectual property
            protection and anti-infringement management. When cooperating with external partners, the Group will require the other party to
            sign the Mutual Confidentiality Agreement , which stipulates the confidentiality scope, rights, obligations, period and liabilities for
            breach of both parties to prevent leakage of intellectual property rights. In the event of a third party using our intellectual property
            without authorization or appropriating our intellectual property, we will take legal actions to protect our own rights and interests.
            During the Year, in response to illegal activities such as piracy and cheating tools, we optimized the infringement monitoring and
            rapid response mechanism. By introducing third-party monitoring tools and conducting internal inspections, we monitored app
            stores, e-commerce platforms, and short video platforms to promptly identify infringement leads. For general infringement, we sent
            batch complaints to invalidate infringing links. For platforms that were unresponsive or uncooperative, we escalated enforcement
            measures appropriately. This included intensifying the crackdown on cheating tools and illegal operations, thereby improving the
            experience for legitimate users and helping to purify the market environment.

            The Group attaches great importance to fostering employees’ awareness of intellectual property compliance and continuously carries
            out education and training on intellectual property rights. During the Year, we conducted scenario-based legal risk control training
            based on actual pain points in our business operations. The training focused on core business scenarios such as font compliance,
            prevention of unpacking and anti-cheating measures, and compliance of user-generated content (UGC). This effectively improved
            employees’ sensitivity to intellectual property compliance in specific business contexts, thereby strengthening internal safeguards.

            During the Year, the number of intellectual property rights maintained by the Group was 2,412, including 1,900 trademarks, 26
            patents, 265 software copyrights, 149 art copyrights, 13 music copyrights, 3 text copyrights and 56 other intellectual works.

     3.5   Integrity in Practice

           The Group strictly complies with the Anti-Money Laundering Law of the People’s Republic of China , the Administrative Measures
           for the Payment Services Provided by Non-Financial Companies , the Detailed Rules for the Implementation of the Administrative
           Measures for the Payment Services Provided by Non-Financial Companies , the Guidelines Against Money Laundering and Terrorist
           Financing through Payment and Clearing Organizations , the Interim Provisions on Prohibiting Commercial Bribery and the Anti-Unfair
           Competition Law and other laws and regulations. We have formulated the Anti-Money Laundering and Anti-Terrorist Measures and
           system and the Anti-Fraud and Anti-Corruption Procedures and Control Manual to set out the measures and handling of illegal acts
           such as bribery, extortion, fraud and money laundering.

           In order to safeguard the legitimate rights and interests of the Company and our employees and foster a fair and transparent working
           environment, we have developed and implemented the Employee Handbook and the Conflict of Interest Management System,
           strictly adhering to the requirements of professional ethics and compliance operations. Regarding conflict of interest management,
           we focus on key scenarios such as investments, external activities, business cooperation and commercial opportunities, personal
           relationships, and personal accounts and financial interests. We have clarified declaration requirements and avoidance rules, defined
           red lines for prohibited conduct, and established corresponding measures for handling violations, forming a standardized process for
           the declaration, assessment, and resolution of conflicts of interest. During the Year, we continued to advance the conflict of interest
           declaration process, employing a combination of semi-annual regular declarations and on-demand declarations. This online process
           covered all employees and achieved a 100% declaration completion rate.

           The Group continuously strengthens integrity and compliance management to prevent risks such as money laundering and fraud.
           Our Employee Handbook explicitly stipulates that employees shall not receive benefits from business-related entities and shall
           not use company resources to conduct insider trading, misappropriate public funds, or engage in similar activities. We maintain a
           “zero-tolerance” stance towards illegal profit-seeking behaviors. We have established an Anti-Money Laundering and Anti-Terrorist
           Financing Steering Group, which meets regularly to review and supervise the progress of the Group’s anti-money laundering and
           anti-terrorist financing work. We adhere to the “Know Your Customer” (KYC) principle, having established and been continuously
           improving our customer identification system. We properly maintain customer information and financial and transaction history
           records related to customers to ensure traceability and verifiability. Aligned with our business characteristics, the Group strictly
           controls risks associated with virtual assets. We prohibit any functions that allow the exchange of game currency, virtual items, or
           accounts for cash and implement strict management and disciplinary measures against violations such as offline trading of virtual
           items.

           The Group has established a formal whistleblowing management mechanism. We have set up a reporting email address:
           jubao@xd.com. The Audit Committee, together with the Internal Audit Department, will analyze, independently follow up on,
           investigate, and provide feedback on reported information. Investigation results are reported to the Board, and relevant personnel
           are dealt with according to regulations; if any law is violated, the matter will be referred to judicial authorities. The Audit Committee
           strictly ensures the confidentiality of the whistleblower’s personal information, effectively protecting their rights and interests.

           During the Year, there were no concluded corruption lawsuits or corruption and fraud cases brought against the Group or its
           employees.

           We continuously enhance our anti-corruption compliance framework. We regularly provide anti-corruption training to our directors
           and employees. Additionally, we disseminate compliance requirements regarding anti-commercial bribery and conflict of interest
           through channels such as emails and internal groups. These efforts persistently raise the compliance awareness of all staff and
           resolutely prevent all types of unlawful conduct.

          3.6   Healthy Gaming

                As a socially responsible enterprise, the Group has always taken protecting minors’ health and preventing game addiction as core
                responsibilities in platform governance and product operations. We strictly abide by laws, regulations, and relevant normative
                documents such as the Network Security Law of the People’s Republic of China , the Law of the People’s Republic of China on
                the Protection of Minors , the Personal Information Protection Law of the People’s Republic of China , the Regulations on the
                Administration of Internet User Account Names , the Regulations on the Administration of Information Services for Internet User
                Public Accounts , the Regulation on the Protection of Minors in Cyberspace , the Notice on Preventing Minors from Being Addicted to
                Online Games , and the Requirements for Age-Appropriate Cues for Online Games . We comprehensively integrate the compliance
                requirements for minor protection into the entire process of platform operation, content management, and product design.

                To consistently foster a healthy and positive gaming and community environment, we strengthen the standardized management of
                platform content, starting with system development and content review. For the TapTap platform, we have formulated the TapTap
                Community Management Regulations , which govern the content posted by platform users, explicitly prohibiting any statements
                involving pornography, undesirable behaviors, illegal or non-compliant activities, or content detrimental to the physical and mental
                health of minors. Concurrently, we have established and implemented the Content Review Guidelines , conducting systematic reviews
                of game content to be launched, preventing the appearance of undesirable behaviors, harmful ideas, or inducing content in games,
                thereby ensuring an overall content style across the platform that is positive, wholesome, and uplifting.

                Centered on the practical needs of minors for safe use of online services, we continuously improve the user guidance and protection
                mechanisms on the TapTap platform specifically for teenagers. The TapTap platform has established and been continuously
                optimizing a “Youth Mode”, which is enabled by default for youth users. The platform gives priority to recommending pure,
                high-quality, content such as knowledge-based, application-based, and science-based materials from the exclusive content pool to
                youth users. Furthermore, the download behaviors of youth users are subject to additional regulations. For games that are confirmed
                as non-compliant with minor protection requirements after review, the platform does not provide download services to any minors.

                While fulfilling its platform responsibilities, TapTap emphasizes collaborative efforts with game developers to jointly build a healthy
                and orderly gaming ecosystem. Recognizing the data security technical challenges and development cost pressures faced by small
                and medium-sized game developers in compliance construction, the TapTap platform launched a self-developed compliance tool.
                This service provides developers with free features such as real-name game authentication, game time limits, and spending limits,
                along with corresponding technical support to assist developers in more efficiently and compliantly implementing anti-addiction
                requirements. Simultaneously, TapTap encourages developers to clearly indicate the appropriate age range for minor users in game
                materials. Upon review and approval, these age-appropriate cues are displayed on the game detail page, further safeguarding the
                right to know and the legitimate rights and interests of young users.

                As a provider of game products, the Group comprehensively implements requirements for minor anti-addiction and spending
                management, starting with product design and operational aspects. We establish relevant policies within our game products to
                prevent harm to the physical and mental health of minors. We require all users to register with real names and record their gaming
                time. All our games are uniformly connected to the National Press and Publication Administration’s real-name verification system
                for online game anti-addiction, and we do not provide any game services to users who have not registered or logged in with their
                real names. We continuously conduct daily inspections of our anti-addiction systems, repairing any vulnerabilities immediately upon
                discovery to ensure timely closed-loop resolution of issues. Meanwhile, we strictly enforce the management of gaming periods
                for minors, providing online game services to minors for one hour only from 20:00 to 21:00 daily on Fridays, Saturdays, Sundays,
                and legal holidays. We also set single transaction and monthly limits for game spending by minors. To further purify the gaming
                environment, we have embedded an AI banned words blocking system in the games to block sensitive and inappropriate content in
                real-time.
52            ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND                                                                                                  XD INC.
                     GOVERNANCE REPORT                                                                                      ANNUAL REPORT 2025




           To consolidate collaborative efforts for minor protection involving the platform, parents, and social supervision, the Group
           continuously improves related mechanisms such as parental guidance and complaint handling. We have prominently launched a
           “Protection of Minors: Parental Guide” page on our official website. This page provides parents with information including policy and
           legal education, gaming time management, behavior settings, health guidance, and customer service contacts, assisting parents in
           more effectively guiding their children to play online games healthily and rationally. Concurrently, the TapTap platform has set up a
           “One-Click Complaint and Reporting” module within the site, including a dedicated reporting option for “content harmful to minors”,
           staffed by dedicated personnel who receive and handle reports 24 hours a day. In September 2025, the Group released the Social
           Responsibility Report on Internet Protection for Minors , systematically summarizing the anti-addiction mechanisms, Youth Mode
           practices, recommendations for high-quality content, and governance achievements, to continuously drive the construction of a safer,
           healthier, and more wholesome online gaming environment.

          3.8   Working Together

                3.8.1 Supplier Management


                       Suppliers are our important partners. The Group’s suppliers include art-related, administrative, and other categories. We
                       have established and implemented systems such as the Procurement and Warehouse Management System , the Fixed Assets
                       Management System , the Administrative Procurement Operation Procedures , the Administrative Procurement Approval
                       Standards , the Supplier Management Standards , and the Green Procurement Guidelines to manage the entire supplier
                       management process.

                       The Group integrates compliance and responsibility requirements throughout the entire supply chain cooperation process.
                       We establish and improve supply chain management and supervision mechanisms to continuously standardize the behavior
                       of our partners. We review the potential environmental and social risks that may be involved in each link of the supply
                       chain and require cooperating suppliers to strictly comply with relevant laws and regulations, strictly prohibiting any form
                       of commercial bribery, unethical conduct, as well as child labor, forced labor, and similar situations. To address the risk of
                       commercial bribery, the Group provides suppliers with the Anti-Bribery Clause , clearly outlining compliance requirements.
                       If a supplier engages in non-compliant behavior, the Group will immediately terminate the cooperative relationship; if such
                       behavior infringes upon the Group’s legitimate rights and interests, we will hold it liable in accordance with the law.

                       In terms of supplier selection and management, the Group utilizes the Supplier Relationship Management (SRM) system to
                       coordinate interactions and data management with suppliers. This system centralizes the recording of supplier information
                       such as operating status, business capabilities, quotations, management level, and quality performance, providing support for
                       comprehensive evaluation and decision-making. The Group conducts regular annual assessments of suppliers, communicating
                       identified shortcomings and following up on rectifications to ensure they consistently meet service and quality requirements.
                       At the procurement execution level, the Group adheres to the principle of comparing multiple suppliers and conducts quality
                       verification for material, software, and hardware procurements, promptly negotiating with suppliers or arranging returns
                       for items that do not meet requirements. Simultaneously, the Group has established a centralized management team to
                       oversee the procurement needs and processes of various departments, enhancing procurement efficiency and coordination.
                       In warehouse management, the principle of “first-in-first-out” is followed, with designated personnel regularly updating the
                       List of Storage Items and specifying storage periods to ensure that warehousing is managed in a standardized and orderly
                       manner.
54     ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND                                                                                                   XD INC.
              GOVERNANCE REPORT                                                                                      ANNUAL REPORT 2025




           Data security is a baseline requirement for supplier cooperation. We have established a comprehensive supplier data
           security management mechanism covering the entire process, from partnership admission and process control to access
           management, to fully safeguard data security. Before cooperation, we sign a confidentiality agreement with the supplier,
           clearly defining mutual confidentiality obligations and prohibiting the unauthorized disclosure, use, or transfer of relevant
           data, thereby protecting trade secrets and sensitive information. When data sharing is involved, we impose additional
           compliance requirements on third parties, such as those handling user personal information through Software Development
           Kits (SDK). These requirements include standardized feedback on data compliance operations, disclosure of relevant content
           in privacy policies, and signing data security standard contractual clauses, ensuring the effective implementation of data
           security measures across the entire cooperation chain. Simultaneously, the Group strictly adheres to the principle of least
           privilege, controlling supplier data access scope so that they can only access data necessary for their work. We also regularly
           review and update access permissions to mitigate the risk of permission misuse. Through the SRM system, we can achieve
           dynamic control over data security. The system monitors data access permissions, records data calls and operations, ensuring
           only authorized personnel can access sensitive data. It also automates compliance checks to ensure suppliers strictly comply
           with the Group’s data security policies.

          The Group actively practices green and low-carbon concepts by formulating and implementing the Green Procurement
          Guidelines . In material procurement, we balance cost considerations with environmental requirements. We prioritize the
          selection of energy-efficient, environmentally friendly, recyclable, and low-pollution products. We are progressively increasing
          the procurement proportion of green products such as low-carbon, renewable, and organic items, while continuously phasing
          out energy-intensive and outdated equipment, thereby reducing the environmental impact of our purchased materials.

           During the Year, the number of suppliers of the Group was 373, among which 113 suppliers were located in Shanghai,
           including 45 art suppliers, 4 administrative suppliers and 64 other suppliers; and 260 suppliers were located in other regions,
           including 107 art suppliers, 1 administrative supplier and 152 other suppliers.

          caring for the elderly, focusing on children’s development, advocating environmental protection concepts, and promoting school-enterprise

          organization has continuously organized employees to participate in various charitable activities, spreading warmth and positive impact.

          in Yunnan Province to provide targeted assistance. On the occasion of the International Children’s Day in the Year, the Group, in collaboration
          with Daning Group’s personnel participating in the Yunnan assistance program, spanned over 2,000 kilometers to deliver “Educational

5. TALENT MANAGEMENT
     Our employees are the Group’s precious assets and the core source of our competitive advantage and innovative drive. The Group strictly
     complies with laws and regulations such as the Labor Law of the People’s Republic of China, the Employment Promotion Law of the People’s
     Republic of China , the Law of the People’s Republic of China on the Protection of Minors , and the Provisions on the Prohibition of Using Child
     Labor , earnestly safeguarding the legitimate rights and interests of employees. We have formulated internal systems such as the Employee
     Handbook , Training Management System , and Recruitment Management System to standardize human resource management processes.

     The Group is committed to creating an equal and inclusive workplace and adopts a “zero-tolerance” stance towards workplace sexual
     harassment, discrimination, and bullying. We adhere to fair and equitable employment principles, and do not discriminate against
     employees based on factors such as race, gender, color, age, family background, ethnic tradition, religion, physical condition, or nationality.
     We ensure that our employees enjoy equal opportunities in recruitment, promotion, compensation, working hours, rest periods, and other
     benefits. During the Year, the Group did not have any cases of non-compliance in relation to compensation, equal opportunities, diversity,
     anti-discrimination and other treatment and benefits of employees.

     5.1    Employment Management


            The Group upholds the principles of fairness and equity in recruitment, continuously improving talent acquisition and management
            mechanisms to ensure a standardized and transparent recruitment process. We have established and implemented the Recruitment
            Management System , clearly defining recruitment-related procedures and management requirements. The Group conducts
            recruitment through diverse and compliant talent acquisition channels, including open recruitment platforms, social platforms, talent
            databases, internal referrals, and human resources service agencies, to extensively attract talents that meet job requirements.
XD INC.                                                                                              ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND                       59
ANNUAL REPORT 2025                                                                                   GOVERNANCE REPORT




            During the recruitment process, the Group conducts necessary qualification and background checks on candidates. If falsified
            resumes or integrity risks are identified, the candidate will not be hired. The selection of candidates will comprehensively take into
            account their professional capabilities, industry experience, job suitability, professional qualities, and alignment with the Group’s
            cultural values, ensuring an open, fair, and equitable recruitment process.

            The Group actively conducts campus recruitment to expand channels for attracting young talents, building a talent reserve for
            the Group’s sustainable development. During the Year, the Group’s campus recruitment efforts reached over 10 universities and
            colleges. The types of institutions covered included art and design academies, animation and game-related specialist colleges,
            and comprehensive key universities. This facilitated the gradual expansion of talent acquisition from specialized institutions to
            comprehensive universities, further enriching the talent structure and level of campus recruitment. Through organizing and
            participating in campus job fairs, conducting campus promotional talks, and hosting visits and exchanges for university students,
            faculty, and teachers, the Group continuously strengthened communication and cooperation with universities, enhancing awareness
            of its employer brand.




                                                           Campus Recruitment Activities

            To further broaden talent acquisition channels and improve talent matching efficiency, the Group has formulated the Reward
            System for Internal Recommendation, encouraging employees to recommend talents that meet job requirements to join us. After a
            successfully recommended new employee joins, the recommending employee can receive a corresponding reward according to the
            relevant rank standards. Simultaneously, to attract outstanding talents with relevant experience and cultural alignment to return, the
            Group has established the Resignation and Reemployment System , and makes corresponding arrangements to the salary level of
            rehired employees based on their position responsibilities and experience.

            The Group lawfully enters into a Labor Contract with employees, clarifying the rights and obligations of both parties, and outlines
            the relevant management requirements in the Employee Handbook . Employees have the right to terminate the labor contract in
            accordance with the law. If an employee confirms their intention to resign, they are required to submit an application in advance
            to their department heads, and after confirmation they must complete the handover of work before leaving. During the employee
            departure process, the Group conducts interviews with the employees to understand their reasons for leaving and related
            suggestions, in order to continuously optimize human resource and employee management mechanisms.

            The Group strictly complies with national laws and regulations concerning labor employment, strictly prohibiting the hiring of child
            labor and any form of forced labor. During the recruitment process, the Group checks employees’ identification documents and, in
            cooperation with third-party recruitment platforms, clearly stipulates the minimum age requirements for hiring employees to ensure
            no child labor is employed. Simultaneously, the Group prohibits any form of forced labor, corporal punishment, confinement, or other
            actions that infringe upon employees’ personal freedom and dignity. If any violations are discovered, investigations will be conducted
            in accordance with relevant laws and regulations, and corresponding corrective measures will be taken.
60      ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND                                                                                                 XD INC.
                GOVERNANCE REPORT                                                                                     ANNUAL REPORT 2025




     During the Year, the Group did not have any cases of violations related to child labor or forced labor.

     The Group has established a standard working hours system, implementing a work arrangement of 5 days per week, 8 hours per
     day, and 40 hours per week. If employees need to work overtime on statutory holidays or rest days due to work requirements, they
     must obtain prior approval from their supervisor in accordance with regulations. On the premise of complying with national and
     local relevant regulations, the Group implements a comprehensive calculation of working hours system or an flexible working hours
     system for certain positions, and has established flexible work arrangements to support employees in achieving work-life balance. The
     Group does not encourage an overtime culture, nor does it view overtime work as a reflection of performance or competitiveness. We
     explicitly oppose the “996” (nine o’clock on, nine o’clock off, work six days per week) work culture and are committed to fostering a
     healthy and sustainable working environment.

          5.2   Employee Care

                The Group has established and been continuously improving its compensation and benefits system. Employee compensation covers
                basic salary, allowances, performance bonuses, year-end bonuses, and stock options, among others. To maintain compensation
                competitiveness, we adjust the salaries of employees with outstanding performance based on the Group’s operating conditions and
                labor market levels. Year-end bonuses are also distributed according to annual operating performance and individual performance.
                Simultaneously, we continue to implement the XD Supplemental Option Program , supporting employees in converting a portion of
                their compensation into company stock options, sharing the outcomes of the Company development with employees.

                The Group strictly pays “Five social insurances and one housing fund”, including pension, medical, maternity, work-related injury,
                unemployment insurance, and the housing provident funds for employees in accordance with laws and regulations. We also ensure
                employees enjoy legally mandated paid annual leave, statutory holidays, maternity leave, pregnancy check-up leave, breastfeeding
                leave, wedding leave, paternity leave, sick leave, work-related injury leave, and other leave-related benefits according to the law.
                Building on this foundation, the Group further improves its benefits system by providing employees with special subsidies such as
                marriage and childbirth subsidies. We also continue to implement the “XD Employee Assistance Program”, offering financial aid to
                employees encountering unexpected accidents, thereby strengthening the safeguard for employees’ lives.

                We continuously enhance workplace care and improve employee support and welfare systems. The Group regularly conducts
                team-building activities such as group meals, afternoon teas, and collective outings to promote team communication and cohesion.
                We also distribute holiday benefits to employees during important festivals to enhance their sense of belonging and the collaborative
                atmosphere, further stimulating work motivation and creativity. During the Year, we installed 4 additional nursing rooms in the office
                areas of various buildings, providing convenient and comfortable dedicated spaces for breastfeeding employees, addressing their
                needs.

     5.3   Health and Safety

           The Group does not involve significant health and safety risks in its daily operations, but we have taken various measures to protect
           the occupational health and safety of our employees. The Group strictly complies with the requirements of regulations such as
           the Fire Protection Law of the People’s Republic of China , conducting comprehensive inspections of fire-fighting equipment in
           office buildings regularly to ensure fire safety facilities are in good working order and usable, creating a safe and reliable working
           environment for employees. We organize employees to participate in fire drills in the campus every year, helping them master the
           use of equipment such as fire extinguishers and fire hydrants, learn fire emergency response procedures, and enhance coordination
           and cooperation, self-rescue, and mutual rescue capabilities in case of fire emergencies. In addition, we regularly conduct health and
           safety emergency training and first-aid training lectures, strengthening employees’ knowledge of occupational health and safety, and
           raising their awareness of safety protection and self-protection. During the Year, the Group conducted 2 safety drills, including a fire
           drill and a riot control drill. The fire drill focused on the practical skills of using fire extinguishers and fire hydrants and fire emergency
           response procedures, helping employees master basic fire-fighting skills. The riot control drill focused on explaining how to wear and
           use riot control equipment, along with riot incident response drills, further strengthening employees’ ability to coordinate, cooperate
           and handle sudden emergencies.




                                      Fire Drill                                                      Riot Control Drill

           The Group values the health protection of employees and their family members, providing annual health check-ups and group
           commercial supplementary medical insurance for employees. We also offer basic medical benefits to employees, their spouses,
           and children. After employees complete their physical examinations, we arrange for professional general practitioners to provide
           one-on-one health consultation services to employees, interpreting the check-up results and offering health guidance, paying
           attention to employees’ individual health needs. In addition, to alleviate issues such as cervical spine discomfort, muscle fatigue,
           and eye strain that employees may experience during work, the Group equips employees with ergonomically designed desks and
           chairs, and sets up rest areas and basic fitness facilities, supporting employees in taking appropriate breaks during work intervals to
           promote physical and mental well-being.

            The Group also cares about the mental health of its employees in work and life, and has launched the XD Tree Hollow EAP Program
            to provide employees with a 24/7 mental health care hotline. The related services cover multiple dimensions including self-growth,
            career development, romantic relationships and family relations, as well as parent-child education, offering employees professional
            psychological support. The Group strictly adheres to the principle of information confidentiality, properly safeguarding the relevant
            information provided by employees during mental health counselling, earnestly respecting and protecting employees’ personal
            privacy, and creating a safe and trustworthy psychological support environment.

            During the Year, the Group did not occur any complaints or lawsuits regarding violations of health and safety-related laws and
            regulations. In the past three years, the Group has not experienced any work-related fatalities. During the Year, the number of days
            lost due to work-related injuries was 16.

            Note: During the Year, the Group experienced 5 work-related injury incidents, of which 4 were traffic accidents occurring during
                  commutes, and 1 involved an employee who slipped and was injured within the office area. In the future, we will implement
                  more comprehensive measures to ensure workplace safety and safeguard employee health and safety.

     5.4   Employee Growth

           The Group attaches great importance to the professional development and skill enhancement of our employees. We have developed
           and have implemented a Training Management Policy that conducts standardized management of training needs assessment and
           curriculum planning to program execution and effectiveness evaluation. This ensures that our training is systematic and effective.
           We have developed a tiered and categorized training framework that is systematically tailored to the growth needs of employees
           at different positions and levels. We conduct regular, diverse training to continuously support our employees in enhancing their
           professional competencies and comprehensive capabilities. To address employees’ varied developmental needs, training curriculum
           of the Group is organized into six categories, including general competencies, professional skills, art and design, operations and
           services, planning, and tool application. Our training programs for the Year include:

           •      Onboarding training for new employees from social recruitment: The Group offers a systematic onboarding program, the
                  “Newcomer Program” for new hires. Delivered through a blend of online and offline sessions, the program covers the Group’s
                  development history, corporate culture, code of conduct, organizational structure, job responsibilities, and talent development
                  and promotion mechanisms. This is designed to help new employees integrate into their teams and adapt to their roles
                  requirements promptly. Upon completion of the training, new recruits are required to take the online “New Employees
                  Should-Know and Should-Master” test, which focuses on foundational compliance areas such as information security. The
                  Group monitors training progress and assessment results of the new employees while regularly collecting feedback. In 2025,
                  both the completion rate and pass rate of the test for new social recruits reached 100%.

           •      Campus recruitment development program: The “SSR Program” is designed for the development of campus recruits, aiming
                  to offer them systematic training and support, help them successfully transition from students to professionals, and enhance
                  their sense of identification with and belonging to the organization. The program focuses on corporate culture, business
                  acumen, and general professional competencies. Aligning with industry trends, it encourages campus recruits to be familiar
                  with AI tools and integrate them into real work scenarios. During the Year, the “SSR Program” achieved an overall participant
                  satisfaction rate of 94.6% and a Net Promoter Score (NPS) of 100%. Campus recruits collectively delivered multiple practical
                  outputs, including participation in the development of several games, some of which received positive market feedback. This
                  further demonstrates the program’s success in combining talent cultivation with practical business.

            •        Open classes and sharing sessions: Through open classes and thematic sharing sessions, the Group builds a learning and
                     exchange platform for all employees facilitating sharing of knowledge and accumulation of experience. These activities focus
                     on the Group’s key businesses and products, core technologies, and cutting-edge industry trends, featuring presentations
                     by industry experts and external professional instructors. This supports employees’ continuous enhancement of professional
                     capabilities and overall competencies. During the Year, the Group prioritized technical sharing and experience exchanges in
                     areas such as AIGC, Unity, and cloud computing. Topics included AI-assisted development, game development, performance
                     optimization, and tool application, driving the application and implementation of new technologies in real business contexts.

            •        Leadership training: The Group launched the “XD Leadership Program,” a leadership training program for newly appointed
                     first-line managers and managers undergoing role transition. The program systematically enhances management capabilities
                     in areas such as goal management, task delegation, progress monitoring, and communication and feedback, facilitating a
                     smooth role transition and improved management effectiveness. By introducing standardized leadership concepts and tools,
                     it helps establish a common management language across teams. During the Year, the program focused on core themes
                     including enhancing role awareness, boosting effective communication (for both individual and team), and high-efficiency
                     execution. A phased approach combining learning with practice was employed to enable participants to transform
                     management principles into actionable practices. During the Year, the program achieved an overall participant satisfaction
                     score of 4.74 out of 5 and a Net Promoter Score (NPS) of 100%.

6. ENVIRONMENTAL PROTECTION
          The Group fully recognizes the importance of environmental protection and resource conservation and strictly complies with the regulations
          of all jurisdictions in which the Group operates, including the Environmental Protection Law of the People’s Republic of China and the Energy
          Conservation Law of the People’s Republic of China and other regulations. As a game developer and online gaming platform operator,
          the Group’s business does not have a substantial impact on the environment, but we take our corporate environmental management
          responsibility seriously and have implemented a number of measures on resource utilization, greenhouse gas emission reduction and
          waste management to demonstrate our commitment and efforts in environmental protection. During the Year, the Group did not violate
          any regulations related to environmental protection or cause any major incidents affecting the environment and natural resources, nor did it
          have any penalties and litigations related to environmental protection.

          The Group set our environmental targets in 2021 and reviews them annually to ensure its reasonableness. As a result of review
          environmental targets of the Group for 2025 are as follows:

          •      Greenhouse gas emissions: take measures to diligently reduce greenhouse gas emissions

          •      Water usage: enhance water-use efficiency and lower water consumption

          •      Low-Carbon travel: encourage low-carbon travel among employees, striving to transition to a low-carbon economy

          6.1    Resource Management


                 The Group integrates green and low-carbon principles deeply into its daily operations. By continuously enhancing resource utilization
                 efficiency, we steadily advance the transition towards green and low-carbon corporate.

                 In terms of resource procurement, we prioritize printing equipment that has the “China Certificate for Energy Conservation ” and the
                 “China Environmental Labelling Product Certificate ”. We also exclusively use paper certified by the Forest Stewardship Council (FSC),
                 effectively minimizing the environmental impact of office consumables.

                 Regarding resource utilization, we implement independent lighting switching and air conditioning regulation systems in each office
                 area, we enable the on-demand switching of lighting and precise temperature control of air conditioning in areas to reduce energy
                 waste on the consumption end. Meanwhile, we enhance water resource management by installing water-saving devices such as
                 automatic sensor taps and regularly measuring and analyzing water usage. Furthermore, we have established a comprehensive
                 annual facility maintenance plan, under which a professional operation and maintenance team conducts monthly inspections of
                 electrical systems, air conditioning units, water supply pipes, and other facilities throughout the building. Any leakage detected is
                 immediately addressed through a repair process, minimizing water loss to the greatest extent possible.

                 For green energy, we have installed photovoltaic panels on the main building of our Shanghai headquarters. By actively promoting
                 solar power generation to partially replace purchased electricity, we further reduce our reliance on fossil fuels and lower the carbon
                 emissions from our operations.
68            ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND                                                                                                   XD INC.
                     GOVERNANCE REPORT                                                                                      ANNUAL REPORT 2025

           The Group sources water from municipal water supply and has no difficulty in sourcing water. Our operations do not involve
           large-scale use of non-renewable energy, forest resources, or impact on biodiversity, etc. Therefore, Aspect A3 Environmental and
           Natural Resources is not applicable to the Group.

     6.2   Waste Management


           The Group’s non-hazardous waste is all domestic waste generated by the offices, which are collected by the property management
           and handed over to the municipal hygiene agency for disposal, so the amount of waste generated cannot be precisely measured.
           We have strictly implemented the policy of waste classification by covering the whole workspace with classified garbage cans and
           equipped them with clear markings and promptly classified and recycled the waste in accordance with the four categories of “Dry
           Waste”, “Wet Waste”, “Recyclables” and “Hazardous Waste”. We also entrust a professional third-party to carry out waste processing,
           promoting the environmental protection and the effective use of the waste. The Group uses environmentally qualified printer rental
           service providers to uniformly provide and recycle relevant equipment consumables in public printing areas. Used toner cartridges
           and ink cartridges in small quantities are recovered and recycled by the recyclers, which has little impact on the environment, and we
           will review the amount of usage and keep statistics on such usage step by step in the future. We have also implemented a paperless
           office by promoting the use of Slack, Confluence and other office software for internal communications or the use of electronic filing
           of documents to reduce the use of paper.

           At the same time, the Group actively practices circular economy and is committed to reducing the use of disposable items. We
           encourage our staff to use more biodegradable products, including reusing eco-bags, replacing plastic cups with paper cups, using
           PLA biodegradable straws, etc., and have stepped up promotional efforts in this regard. The Group’s coffee bars have also launched
           corresponding initiatives by offering discounts to employees who purchase beverages with their own cups. A coffee grounds recycling
           program has also been launched, with collection points set up in buildings and coffee bars for employees to receive free coffee
           grounds for use in home gardening or maintenance items, thus realizing the effective reuse of waste materials.
XD INC.                                                                                                ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND                   69
ANNUAL REPORT 2025                                                                                     GOVERNANCE REPORT




                                                     Coffee Grounds Recycling and Reuse


                Due to the nature of our business, the Group does not use packaging materials.

          6.3   Pollutant Management


                The Group uses electric vehicles and has no vehicle fuel usage; therefore, the Group does not generate nitrogen oxides, sulfur oxides,
                or particulate matters. If such emission occurs in the future, we will regularly review the emission of pollutants and closely monitor
                the concentration of pollutant emissions to ensure that the emissions follow the relevant regulatory requirements. We regularly
                engage a third-party organization to conduct tests on our canteen fume purification equipment and oil-water separators in Shanghai
                office, and the test results follow the standard requirements. We also regularly clean the fume ducts of our canteen kitchens to
                reduce generation of pollutants and to ensure that the equipment is in optimal working condition.
70            ENVIRONMENTAL, SOCIAL AND                                                                                                   XD INC.
                     GOVERNANCE REPORT                                                                                      ANNUAL REPORT 2025




     6.4   Green Lifestyle

           Beyond continuously promoting energy conservation, water saving, and waste control in daily operations, the Group also focuses
           on implementing low-carbon practices in areas most relevant to employees’ daily routines, such as catering and commuting. In our
           catering aspect, the Group promotes the use of green and healthy food in our canteens and prioritizes the procurement of locally
           produced food to reduce carbon emissions from transportation. During the Reporting Period, the Group’s cafeteria was certified
           as the “Green Restaurant”. In commuting aspect, Group advocates green travel and encourages employees to prioritize public
           transportation and renewable energy vehicles for business travel, such as employee business trips and corporate vehicles usage. We
           provide staff shuttle buses to and from the office and metro stations and encourage our staff to choose carpooling and use more
           public transportation for daily commutes and replace partial business trips with online meetings in order to reduce fuel usage and
           relevant carbon emissions.




                                                    “Green Restaurant” Certificate

     6.5   Response to Climate Change


           Concerned about the impact of climate change on the Group’s value chain, we take the initiative in responding to the national “Carbon
           Peaking” and “Carbon Neutrality” goals. Specifically, we have clarified our responsibilities for addressing climate change, taking into
           account the nature of our business, and identified climate risks and opportunities. We are also continuing to improve our climate
           change management and enhance our ability to respond to climate change.

           Governance


           We have incorporated climate-related management responsibilities into our ESG governance framework. The Board of Directors is
           the ultimate governance body for overseeing our climate-related matters, with the support of the Audit Committee. Management
           is responsible for the overall management of these matters, the ESG Working Group ensures coordinated progress in climate
           management, and the executive departments are responsible for implementing the specific climate management tasks. During the
           Reporting Period, we invited a third-party institution to conduct our Board of Directors in climate-related training, equipping them
           with the capability to govern these issues.

           Note: For specific governance responsibilities at all levels, please refer to “2.2 ESG Governance Structure”.

     Note:

     1.      Our core business is developing and operating games, and our assets primarily comprise office and IT equipment. We do not
             have any significant assets or production activities with high emissions that are highly sensitive to climate-related physical or
             transition risks. Therefore, can reasonably predict that in the short, medium and long term, there will be no climate-related
             risks and opportunities that will have a substantial impact on our financial position, financial performance, cash flows,
             financing channels, capital costs, business model (including resource allocation) or operations. In accordance with the “comply
             or explain” provisions of the ESG Reporting Code (Appendix D), we are not required to conduct a quantitative analysis of our
             current financial impact.

     2.      Based on the assessment, the Group anticipates that climate-related risks and opportunities will have limited impact on
             its business operations and value chain in the short, medium and long term. Therefore, the Group has not developed any
             climate-related transforming plans.

     3.      Following a thorough evaluation of the existing data foundation and analytical capabilities, the Group concluded that
             undertaking climate scenario analysis would incur undue costs or effort. Consequently, during the Reporting Period, the
             Group adopted “reasonable information relief” for climate scenario analysis. The Group commits to enhancing its ability
             to cope with climate-related risks through measures such as optimizing operations and management, and strengthening
             energy conservation and emission reduction. We will also adjust the disclosure arrangements in accordance with regulatory
             disclosure requirements and internal conditions in the future.

     Risk Management


     We make climate-related risk and opportunity management part of our overall risk management process. We have developed a
     comprehensive system for managing climate-related risks and opportunities, consisting of identifying, assessing, responding to and
     monitoring these risks and opportunities. This has fully enhanced our ability to address climate change.

            Metrics and Targets

            To continuously reduce carbon emissions generated by our operations, we carry out regular greenhouse gas emission accounting
            and analysis each year. This allows us to identify the main sources of emissions and strengthen our carbon emission management.
            Our Scope 1 greenhouse gas emissions are mainly produced by our use of natural gas, whereas our Scope 2 greenhouse gas
            emissions are primarily generated by our use of purchased electricity.

            During the Year, the Group’s greenhouse gas emissions and intensity were as follows:

            Greenhouse Gas Emissions                                                          Unit                                            2025


            Greenhouse gas emissions (Scope 1)                                                tCO2e                                          85.77
            Greenhouse gas emissions (Scope 2) (Location-based)                               tCO2e                                       3,107.60
            Total GHG Emissions (Scope 1 and 2)                                               tCO2e                                       3,193.37
            Greenhouse gas emission intensity (per square meter)                              tCO2e/m2                                        0.13
              (Scope 1 and 2)
            GHG emissions per capita (Scope 1 and 2)                                          tCO2e/person                                    2.08


            Notes:

            1.       The scope of the Group’s environmental KPI covered the head office in Shanghai and Shanghai Branch (Zizhu Park), Shenzhen
                     Branch, and Singapore Branch.

            2.       The Scope 1 and Scope 2 emissions are calculated in accordance with the GHG Protocol and the GHG emission is presented in
                     carbon dioxide equivalents. Among them, Scope 2 GHG emissions from the use of purchased electricity (China) are calculated
                     using the national average grid emission factor for 2023 (0.5306 tCO2/MWh), as announced by the Ministry of Ecology and
                     Environment. GHG emissions from the use of purchased electricity (Singapore) are calculated using the grid emission factor
                     for 2024 (0.402 tCO2/MWh), as announced by the Energy Market Authority.

            3.       Due to the nature of our business, the Scope 3 GHG emissions during the Reporting Period were not identified as having a
                     significant impact. As using our actual internal data to calculate Scope 3 emissions would incur extremely high information
                     collection costs to obtain all reasonable and substantiated information, “reasonable information relief” was adopted during
                     the Reporting Period.

            4.       Given the nature of our business, we have not identified any climate-related risks or opportunities that necessitate additional
                     capital expenditure, financing or investment arrangements.

            5.       As calculating cross-industry and industry-specific climate disclosure indicators would incur extremely high information
                     collection costs to obtain all reasonable and substantiated information, “reasonable information relief” was adopted during
                     the Reporting Period.

            6.       We have not yet established an internal carbon pricing mechanism or incorporated climate factors into our remuneration and
                     incentive system (for directors and employees). We have also not set quantifiable climate management targets. In the future,
                     we will continuously monitor updates to climate disclosure requirements, taking into account regulatory developments,
                     the realities of our business and our internal management capacity building. We will optimize the disclosure content and
                     arrangements as needed.
